Abdullahi also condemned Iran's strikes on its neighbours.

  [^cite_note-321]

-  [South Africa](17416221.md): Without mentioning any involved parties by name, on 28 February, president [Cyril Ramaphosa](1089194.md) called on the international community to "redouble efforts" regarding restraint and adherence to international law in the Middle East, with specific reference to [Article 51 of the UN Charter](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Article_51_of_the_UN_Charter).

  [^cite_note-Post-297]

-  [South Sudan](32350676.md): The Ministry of Foreign Affairs issued a statement condemning the Iranian attacks on Gulf states, Turkey, Cyprus, and Azerbaijan, expressing solidarity to those countries and calling the Iranian strikes a violation of international law.

  [^cite_note-322]

  &#x20;South Sudan later cosponsored [United Nations Security Council Resolution 2817](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/United_Nations_Security_Council_Resolution_2817), condemning Iran's retaliatory attacks on the Gulf states.

  [^cite_note-\:0-305]

-  [Sudan](27421.md): President [Abdel Fattah al-Burhan](60480545.md) condemned the Iranian strikes on Gulf states, warning that the [Sudanese Armed Forces](27429.md) (SAF) would confront a radical group within the SAF calling to support Iran in case of a ground invasion.

  [^cite_note-sudanburhan-323]

  &#x20;Al-Burhan said that Sudan "stands in full solidarity" with its Gulf allies and added that he will not allow any group to speak in the name of SAF on matters that "do not concern them."

  [^cite_note-sudanburhan-323]

-  [Togo](30138.md): Togo cosponsored [United Nations Security Council Resolution 2817](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/United_Nations_Security_Council_Resolution_2817), condemning Iran's retaliatory attacks on the Gulf states.

  [^cite_note-\:0-305]

-  [Tunisia](30188.md): The Tunisian foreign ministry expressed its "absolute rejection" of any aggression against Arab countries and expressed full solidarity with Saudi Arabia, Kuwait, Qatar, Bahrain, the United Arab Emirates, Jordan, and Iraq.

  [^cite_note-324]

  &#x20;Tunisia later cosponsored [United Nations Security Council Resolution 2817](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/United_Nations_Security_Council_Resolution_2817), condemning Iran's retaliatory attacks on the Gulf states.

  [^cite_note-\:0-305]

-  [Uganda](31816.md): The Chief of Defence Forces [Muhoozi Kainerugaba](29207362.md), who is a son of President [Yoweri Museveni](293409.md), stated on 26 March that Uganda will join the war "on the side of Israel" if the conflict does not end.

  [^cite_note-325]

  &#x20;He added that Iran must stop attacks on Israel, citing Israel's right to exist.

  [^cite_note-jpostuganda-270]

  &#x20;Kainerugaba also said that Tehran "could have fallen within 72 hours without a bombing", but that they "never listen to a black man."

  [^cite_note-jpostuganda-270]

  &#x20;Uganda later cosponsored [United Nations Security Council Resolution 2817](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/United_Nations_Security_Council_Resolution_2817), condemning Iran's retaliatory attacks on the Gulf states.

  [^cite_note-\:0-305]

-  [Zambia](34415.md): The government co-sponsored a Bahrain-led [UN Security Council](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/UN_Security_Council) resolution condemning Iranian strikes on its neighbours, calling them "egregious."

  [^cite_note-326]

#### Asia

-  [Afghanistan](737.md): The [foreign ministry](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Afghan_Foreign_Ministry) said that recent US and Israeli strikes on Iran would have "long-term negative consequences" for the region, as hostilities continued to escalate (the Taliban regime is also fighting a concurrent [war](82482902.md) against Pakistan at the same time). In a statement, the [Taliban](30635.md) expressed what it called "deep regret" over the attacks on Iran.

  [^cite_note-327]

  &#x20;Foreign Minister [Amir Khan Muttaqi](68480375.md) denounced the aggression against Iran and extended condolences to the Iranian government and people over the targeting of Khamenei.

  [^cite_note-328]

-  [Armenia](10918072.md): The government and foreign ministry condemned Israel's "unilateral attack", calling it "deeply concerning".

  [^cite_note-oc-298]

  

  Azerbaijani president [Ilham Aliyev](22765442.md) visiting the Iranian Embassy in Baku to offer condolences for the passing of Ayatollah Ali Khamenei, 4 March 2026

-  [Azerbaijan](746.md): In an official statement, the [Ministry of Foreign Affairs](11712667.md) called on all parties to exercise maximum restraint and return to the negotiating table. The Ministry emphasized the importance of respecting the sovereignty, territorial integrity, and independence of all states in accordance with the UN Charter.

  [^cite_note-329]

-  [Bangladesh](3454.md): In an official statement, the [Foreign Ministry](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Ministry_of_Foreign_Affairs_\(Bangladesh\)) expressed deep concern over the safety of [Bangladeshis in the Middle East](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Bangladeshis_in_the_Middle_East), and called all parties to exercise maximum restrain and return to diplomacy to resolve the differences.

  [^cite_note-330]

  &#x20;The statement also condemned Iran's retaliatory strikes as "violation of sovereignty" of Gulf countries. The statement was criticized as "one-sided" for not condemning Israel and the US.

  [^cite_note-331]

  &#x20;The following day, the foreign ministry condemned the assassination of Khamenei, calling it a "violation of international law and norms."

  [^cite_note-332]

  &#x20;Bangladesh later cosponsored [United Nations Security Council Resolution 2817](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/United_Nations_Security_Council_Resolution_2817), condemning Iran's retaliatory attacks on the Gulf states.

  [^cite_note-\:0-305]

-  [Bhutan](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Bhutan): Bhutan cosponsored [United Nations Security Council Resolution 2817](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/United_Nations_Security_Council_Resolution_2817), condemning Iran's retaliatory attacks on the Gulf states.

  [^cite_note-\:0-305]

-  [Brunei](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Brunei): The Ministry of Foreign Affairs "strongly condemned" the United States and Israeli attacks on Iran, calling them a serious violation of sovereignty and a threat to regional stability.

  [^cite_note-333]

  &#x20;Brunei later cosponsored [United Nations Security Council Resolution 2817](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/United_Nations_Security_Council_Resolution_2817), condemning Iran's retaliatory attacks on the Gulf states.

  [^cite_note-\:0-305]

-  [Cambodia](334751.md): The Foreign Ministry called on all parties to exercise maximum restraint to avoid further escalation that would harm civilian lives and undermine peace."

  [^cite_note-diplomatc\&s-334]

-  [China](5405.md): The [foreign ministry](7453497.md) expressed concerns about the US and Israeli strikes, called for an immediate end to hostilities against Iran, and to resume dialogue. It also stressed that Iranian sovereignty must be respected.

  [^cite_note-335]

  &#x20;Foreign Minister [Wang Yi](18211536.md) condemned the attack.

  [^cite_note-336]

  &#x20;On 1 March, the foreign ministry stated that the killing of Khamenei is "a grave violation of Iran's sovereignty and security", which tramples on the purposes and principles of the UN Charter and basic norms in international relations.

  [^cite_note-337]

-  [Georgia](48768.md): Georgia cosponsored [United Nations Security Council Resolution 2817](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/United_Nations_Security_Council_Resolution_2817), condemning Iran's retaliatory attacks on the Gulf states.

  [^cite_note-\:0-305]

-  [India](14533.md): The [Ministry for External Affairs](10302410.md) expressed its concerns and requested all three nations for a ceasefire.

  [^cite_note-338]

  &#x20;Prime Minister [Narendra Modi](444222.md), spoke with Gulf leaders

  [^cite_note-339]

  &#x20;and condemned the strikes and attack on sovereignty without mentioning Iran.

  [^cite_note-340]

  &#x20;Modi also spoke with the Israeli PM.

  [^cite_note-341]

  &#x20;The Indian government did not condemn the assassination of Khamenei, just like in similar previous cases.

  [^cite_note-342]

  &#x20;However, [Foreign Secretary](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Foreign_Secretary_\(India\)) [Vikram Misri](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Vikram_Misri) signed a condolence book a few days later without mentioning any background of Khamenei's demise.

  [^cite_note-343]

  &#x20;India later cosponsored [United Nations Security Council Resolution 2817](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/United_Nations_Security_Council_Resolution_2817), condemning Iran's retaliatory attacks on the Gulf states.

  [^cite_note-\:0-305]

-  [Indonesia](14579.md): The [Ministry of Foreign Affairs](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Ministry_of_Foreign_Affairs_\(Indonesia\)) issued a statement regretting the failure of negotiations between Iran and the United States.

  [^cite_note-344]

  &#x20;In a call to Iranian Foreign Minister [Abbas Araghchi](21951274.md), Indonesian Foreign Minister [Sugiono](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Sugiono) stated President [Prabowo Subianto](2829084.md)'s willingness to travel to Tehran in an attempt to mediate the conflict.

  [^cite_note-345]

  &#x20;Amid escalating Middle East tensions, Indonesia paused its engagement with the US-initiated [Board of Peace](81274522.md), as domestic calls for withdrawal intensified following the US-Israel attacks on Iran.

  [^cite_note-346]

-  [Japan](15573.md): Prime Minister [Sanae Takaichi](8125532.md) announced on [X](9988187.md) that "we will prepare for any potential risks, and handle it in the best possible way that we can."

  [^cite_note-347]

  [^cite_note-348]

  &#x20;Takaichi also emphasized during the [House of Representatives](217778.md) Budget Committee that "Japan's consistent position is that Iran's development of nuclear weapons can never be tolerated" and urged Iran to cease hostilities. She did not make any statements over the actions taken by Israel and the United States.

  [^cite_note-349]

  [^cite_note-350]

  &#x20;Defense Minister [Shinjirō Koizumi](27407451.md) also announced that preparations were being made to evacuate Japanese expats in the region by the [Self-Defense Forces](2785204.md).

  [^cite_note-351]

  &#x20;On 5 March, Prime Minister Takaichi condemned the Iranian attacks against civilian facilities.

  [^cite_note-352]

  &#x20;As of 5 April, the government has refrained from assessing whether the US-Israeli attacks violate international law.

  [^cite_note-Nikkei-2026-103]

  &#x20;Japan later cosponsored [United Nations Security Council Resolution 2817](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/United_Nations_Security_Council_Resolution_2817), condemning Iran's retaliatory attacks on the Gulf states.

  [^cite_note-\:0-305]

-  [Kazakhstan](16642.md): President [Kassym-Jomart Tokayev](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Kassym-Jomart_Tokayev) ordered the [Security Council](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Security_Council_of_Kazakhstan) to ensure domestic stability following the strikes, while the [ministry of foreign affairs](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Ministry_of_Foreign_Affairs_\(Kazakhstan\)) expressed solidarity with Gulf states and condemned Iranian retaliatory actions.

  [^cite_note-353]

  [^cite_note-354]

-  [Laos](17752.md): The Ministry of Foreign Affairs expressed "grave concern" over the escalating violence in the Middle East, urging those involved to exercise restraint and seek a peaceful solution.

  [^cite_note-355]

-  [Malaysia](3607937.md): Malaysia officially condemned the joint U.S.-Israeli strikes on Iran and retaliatory Iranian strikes in the region, calling for de-escalation. Prime Minister [Anwar Ibrahim](19264815.md) expressed a stronger stance, "unreservedly" condemning the assassination of Ali Khamenei, describing it as a "vile attempt" to destroy regional stability. He further accused Israel of lacking humanity and moved to table a parliamentary motion to condemn the attack.

  [^cite_note-diplomatc\&s-334]

  &#x20;Malaysia later cosponsored [United Nations Security Council Resolution 2817](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/United_Nations_Security_Council_Resolution_2817), condemning Iran's retaliatory attacks on the Gulf states.

  [^cite_note-\:0-305]

-  [Maldives](19117.md): President [Mohamed Muizzu](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Mohamed_Muizzu) condemned attacks by all sides while expressing support for the Arab nations attacked by Iran.

  [^cite_note-356]

  &#x20;The Maldives later cosponsored [United Nations Security Council Resolution 2817](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/United_Nations_Security_Council_Resolution_2817), condemning Iran's retaliatory attacks on the Gulf states.

  [^cite_note-\:0-305]

-  [Myanmar](19457.md): Myanmar cosponsored [United Nations Security Council Resolution 2817](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/United_Nations_Security_Council_Resolution_2817), condemning Iran's retaliatory attacks on the Gulf states.

  [^cite_note-\:0-305]

-  [North Korea](21255.md): A spokesperson for the [Ministry of Foreign Affairs](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Ministry_of_Foreign_Affairs_\(North_Korea\)) condemned Israel's and the United States' attacks as "illegal aggression" that "constitute the most heinous form of violation of sovereignty". The spokesperson added that "the Democratic People's Republic of Korea condemns in the strongest terms the shameless and gangster-like behaviour of the United States and Israel, who do not hesitate to abuse military force to achieve their selfish and hegemonic ambitions, placing domestic law above recognized international law", stating the "war of aggression" cannot be justified.

  [^cite_note-357]

  [^cite_note-358]

-  [Pakistan](23235.md): Pakistan's Deputy Prime Minister and Foreign Minister [Ishaq Dar](988764.md) condemned the Israeli and American strikes on Iran.

  [^cite_note-359]

  &#x20;He also stated he reminded Iran of the [mutual defense agreement](81115024.md) they signed with Saudi Arabia in September 2025.

  [^cite_note-360]

  [^cite_note-361]

  &#x20;Pakistan later cosponsored [United Nations Security Council Resolution 2817](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/United_Nations_Security_Council_Resolution_2817), condemning Iran's retaliatory attacks on the Gulf states.

  [^cite_note-\:0-305]

-  [Philippines](23440.md): President [Bongbong Marcos](572460.md) called for a ceasefire in the Middle East and expressed support for "the countries that have come under attack".

  [^cite_note-362]

-  [Singapore](27318.md): The Ministry of Foreign Affairs said that it "regrets the failure of negotiations", calling on parties involved to return to negotiations to achieve a peaceful solution", citing international law and the UN charter.

  [^cite_note-diplomatc\&s-334]

  &#x20;Singapore later cosponsored [United Nations Security Council Resolution 2817](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/United_Nations_Security_Council_Resolution_2817), condemning Iran's retaliatory attacks on the Gulf states.

  [^cite_note-\:0-305]

-  [South Korea](27019.md): South Korea cosponsored [United Nations Security Council Resolution 2817](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/United_Nations_Security_Council_Resolution_2817), condemning Iran's retaliatory attacks on the Gulf states.

  [^cite_note-\:0-305]

-  [Thailand](30128.md) said it was "closely monitoring the situation with grave concern."

  [^cite_note-363]

  &#x20;The [National Human Rights Commission of Thailand](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/National_Human_Rights_Commission_of_Thailand) condemned the attacks on Iran, particularly the [Minab school attack](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Minab_school_attack).

  [^cite_note-364]

-  [Timor-Leste](19159399.md): Timor-Leste cosponsored [United Nations Security Council Resolution 2817](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/United_Nations_Security_Council_Resolution_2817), condemning Iran's retaliatory attacks on the Gulf states.

  [^cite_note-\:0-305]

-  [Turkmenistan](198149.md): The government of Turkmenistan, which adheres to a strong neutral international position, called the outbreak of the war "regrettable" and called for a diplomatic solution to solve the problem.

  [^cite_note-365]

  &#x20;The government of Turkmenistan, one of the most strict countries in [travel visa](330265.md) regulations, opened safe ways for foreigners in Iran, especially Russians, to enter Turkmenistan as a route of evacuation.

  [^cite_note-366]

-  [Uzbekistan](31853.md): The [ministry of foreign affairs](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Ministry_of_Foreign_Affairs_\(Uzbekistan\)) expressed "serious concern" over the escalation of hostilities, urging all parties to exercise maximum restraint and seek a diplomatic resolution to prevent further regional instability.

  [^cite_note-367]

  &#x20;Uzbekistan later cosponsored [United Nations Security Council Resolution 2817](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/United_Nations_Security_Council_Resolution_2817), condemning Iran's retaliatory attacks on the Gulf states.

  [^cite_note-\:0-305]

-  [Vietnam](202354.md): The spokesperson of the [Ministry of Foreign Affairs](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Ministry_of_Foreign_Affairs_\(Vietnam\)) formally reiterated the state's opposition to the use of force against [sovereign nations](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Nation_state), without explicitly mentioning any side of the conflict.

  [^cite_note-368]

##### Middle East

-  [Bahrain](18933277.md): Bahrain condemned the Iranian strikes on the headquarters of the [US Navy Fifth Fleet](418867.md) which it hosts, calling it a "treacherous attack" and a "blatant violation of the kingdom's sovereignty and security".

  [^cite_note-Al_Jazeera_English-2-369]

  &#x20;According to diplomats, Bahrain privately supports a U.S. ground invasion of Iran.

  [^cite_note-gulfallies-370]

  &#x20;Bahrain later cosponsored [United Nations Security Council Resolution 2817](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/United_Nations_Security_Council_Resolution_2817), condemning Iran's retaliatory attacks on the Gulf states.

  [^cite_note-\:0-305]

-  [Iraq](7515928.md): The spokesperson for the [Iraqi Prime Minister](208879.md) condemned the assassination of Ali Khamenei and declared three days of mourning for him.

  [^cite_note-371]

  &#x20;Iraqi Prime Minister [Mohammed Shia' al-Sudani](37393258.md) condemned US-Israeli strikes on the [PMF](45671374.md), calling them a "systematic and repeated aggression" and a "desperate attempt to cause chaos and hit social peace." Iraqi national security advisor [Qasim al-Araji](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Qasim_al-Araji) condemned the strikes on the PMF as a "cowardly terrorist attack."

  [^cite_note-372]

  &#x20;In response to plans of a [Kurdish offensive into Iran](82575260.md), al-Araji said that Iraq will not allow an invasion of Iran to be launched from its territory, and stated that Iran had requested that Iraqi forces move to the border between the Iraqi Kurdistan and Iran to impede any aggression by Kurdish forces.

  [^cite_note-373]

  &#x20;On 26 March, Iraq condemned the [attacks on Gulf Arab states and Jordan](82751620.md).

  [^cite_note-374]

-  [Jordan](7515964.md): The foreign ministry of Jordan strongly condemned the Iranian ballistic missile on Jordanian territory as well as the attacks targeting Gulf Arab states, with whom it expressed its "absolute solidarity," further adding that it would take "all available and necessary measures" to safeguard its citizens and protect its security and sovereignty.

  [^cite_note-375]

  &#x20;Jordan later cosponsored [United Nations Security Council Resolution 2817](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/United_Nations_Security_Council_Resolution_2817), condemning Iran's retaliatory attacks on the Gulf states.

  [^cite_note-\:0-305]

-  [Kuwait](7515890.md): Kuwait condemned the Iranian attacks on its territory as a "flagrant violation" of international law.

  [^cite_note-Al_Jazeera_English-2026a-256]

  &#x20;Later Kuwait's [Emir Sheikh Mishal Al-Ahmad Al-Jaber Al-Sabah](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Mishal_Al-Ahmad_Al-Jaber_Al-Sabah) said his country is under attack by a Muslim country they consider a friend, even though Kuwait did not allow the use of our land, airspace, or waters for any military action against it.

  [^cite_note-376]

  &#x20;According to diplomats, Kuwait privately supports a U.S. ground invasion of Iran.

  [^cite_note-gulfallies-370]

  &#x20;Kuwait later cosponsored [United Nations Security Council Resolution 2817](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/United_Nations_Security_Council_Resolution_2817), condemning Iran's retaliatory attacks on the Gulf states.

  [^cite_note-\:0-305]

  &#x20;On May 13, *[The Wall Street Journal](173070.md)* reported that Kuwait had launched attacks on Iran-backed Iraqi militias,

  [^cite_note-377]

  &#x20;and Kuwait stated that it had engaged Iranian forces during a [raid on Bubiyan Island](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/2026_Bubiyan_Island_raid).

  [^cite_note-378]

-  [Lebanon](17771.md): Prime Minister [Nawaf Salam](21077090.md) urged restraint following the Israeli strikes on Iran, stressing that Lebanon must not be dragged into a wider regional war and should prioritize its own security and stability.

  [^cite_note-Apa.az-299]

  [^cite_note-LBCIV7-300]

  [^cite_note-Al_Jazeera_English-2026a-256]

  &#x20;Salam also condemned Hezbollah's decision to [join the war by attacking Israel](82549840.md), calling it "an irresponsible and suspicious act that jeopardizes Lebanon's security and safety and provides Israel with pretexts to continue its aggression".

  [^cite_note-379]

  &#x20;He also said "We will not allow the country to be dragged into new adventures".

  [^cite_note-380]

  &#x20;In a later interview he said "We could have avoided being impacted by the conflict were it not for the strategic error committed by Hezbollah by being dragged us into it."

  [^cite_note-381]

  &#x20;Lebanon later cosponsored [United Nations Security Council Resolution 2817](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/United_Nations_Security_Council_Resolution_2817), condemning Iran's retaliatory attacks on the Gulf states.

  [^cite_note-\:0-305]

-  [Oman](22316.md): Foreign Minister [Badr Albusaidi](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Badr_Albusaidi) expressed "dismay" at the outbreak of violence in spite of the [2026 Iran–United States negotiations](79685207.md), urging the United States to "not get sucked in further" into the conflict, adding "this is not your war."

  [^cite_note-Al_Jazeera_English-2-369]

  &#x20;A spokesperson for the Foreign Minister condemned the strikes, calling them illegal aggression. Oman also called for a [UN Security council](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/UN_Security_council) meeting.

  [^cite_note-382]

  [^cite_note-383]

  &#x20;Badr Albusaidi claimed the United States had "lost control of its own foreign policy" and accused Israel of persuading the Trump administration to engage in a war with Iran, which he termed a "grave miscalculation" and a "catastrophe".

  [^cite_note-384]

  &#x20;Oman later cosponsored [United Nations Security Council Resolution 2817](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/United_Nations_Security_Council_Resolution_2817), condemning Iran's retaliatory attacks on the Gulf states.

  [^cite_note-\:0-305]

-  [Palestine](241405.md): The [Palestinian Authority](24093.md) condemned the Iranian retaliatory strikes on the [Gulf Arab states](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Gulf_Arab_states), stating that it "affirms the solidarity of our people and their steadfastness alongside the sister Arab nations" and expressed support for "whatever measures" they take to defend themselves.

  [^cite_note-385]

-  [Qatar](8238258.md): The foreign ministry strongly condemned Iran for launching missiles at Qatari territory, calling it a violation of Qatar's sovereignty and reaffirming its right to respond.

  [^cite_note-Al_Jazeera_English-2026a-256]

  &#x20;On 19 March, Qatar declared members of the Iranian military and security forces as 'persona non grata', giving them 24 hours to leave the country.

  [^cite_note-386]

  &#x20;Qatar later cosponsored [United Nations Security Council Resolution 2817](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/United_Nations_Security_Council_Resolution_2817), condemning Iran's retaliatory attacks on the Gulf states.

  [^cite_note-\:0-305]

-  [Saudi Arabia](349303.md): The [foreign ministry](31639195.md) condemned what it called "blatant and cowardly Iranian attacks" on [Riyadh](51554.md) and [Eastern Province](468721.md), highlighting that the attacks took place in spite of the explicit closure of its airspace.

  [^cite_note-387]

  &#x20;In a statement released by the official press agency, Saudi Arabia condemned the "flagrant" and "brutal Iranian aggression" on its fellow Arab states of the UAE, Bahrain, Qatar, Kuwait, and Jordan, stating that it was a "blatant violation" of their sovereignty.

  [^cite_note-388]

  [^cite_note-389]

  [^cite_note-390]

  &#x20;Saudi Arabia claimed to have intercepted all Iranian attacks aimed at its territory and said that it would "take all necessary measures" to defend itself, "including the option of responding to aggression".

  [^cite_note-391]

  &#x20;Saudi Crown Prince [Mohammed bin Salman](38689358.md) urged Trump to "keep hitting the Iranians hard."

  [^cite_note-392]

  [^cite_note-393]

  &#x20;Saudi Arabia later cosponsored [United Nations Security Council Resolution 2817](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/United_Nations_Security_Council_Resolution_2817), condemning Iran's retaliatory attacks on the Gulf states.

  [^cite_note-\:0-305]

  &#x20;In May, *[Reuters](18998750.md)* reported that Saudi Arabia had launched attacks on both Iran and Iran-backed militias within Iraq.

  [^cite_note-394]

-  [Syria](7515849.md): The [Ministry of Foreign Affairs](19972797.md) said it "strongly condemns the Iranian attacks" that targeted Saudi Arabia, the United Arab Emirates, Bahrain, Qatar, Kuwait and Jordan.

  [^cite_note-395]

  &#x20;Syria later cosponsored [United Nations Security Council Resolution 2817](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/United_Nations_Security_Council_Resolution_2817), condemning Iran's retaliatory attacks on the Gulf states.

  [^cite_note-\:0-305]

-  [Turkey](11125639.md): President [Recep Tayyip Erdogan](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Recep_Tayyip_Erdo%C4%9Fan) condemned both the U.S.–Israeli strikes on Iran and the Iranian retaliation on its neighbors. He also expressed his "sadness" at the [assassination of Ali Khamenei](82537558.md) and offered his condolences to the Iranian people.

  [^cite_note-396]

  &#x20;He later said the Israeli-US attacks were "illegal" and constituted a "clear violation of international law".

  [^cite_note-397]

-  [United Arab Emirates](69328.md): The defense ministry condemned the Iranian attacks on its territory in the "strongest terms", calling the attacks "a dangerous escalation and a cowardly act that threatens the security and safety of civilians", and stating that it has the "full right" to respond.

  [^cite_note-Al_Jazeera_English-2026a-256]

  &#x20;The UAE also closed its embassy in Tehran and withdrew all its diplomatic mission.

  [^cite_note-398]

  &#x20;UAE ambassador to the U.S. [Yousef al-Otaiba](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Yousef_Al_Otaiba) rejected a ceasefire as "not enough" and called on the U.S. to achieve a "conclusive outcome" against Iran, addressing Iran's "full range of threats: nuclear capabilities, missiles, drones, terror proxies and blockades of international sea lanes."

  [^cite_note-399]

  &#x20;According to diplomats, the UAE emerged as the most hawkish of the Gulf states and pushed Trump to launch a ground invasion of Iran.

  [^cite_note-gulfallies-370]

-  [Yemen](350939.md): The Yemeni foreign ministry condemned the Iranian targeting of oil and gas infrastructure in Saudi Arabia and other GCC states and full solidarity with the Gulf Arab states.

  [^cite_note-400]

  &#x20;Following the [Houthi entry into the war](82820319.md) on 28 March, the internationally recognized [Yemeni government](70488867.md) condemned Iran's "frequent attempts to drag Yemen" into conflict "through its terrorist militias," adding that "the involvement of the Houthi militias in defending the Iranian regime" shows that Iran is "pushing its agents to open other fronts" to reduce political pressure on itself.

  [^cite_note-401]

  &#x20;Yemen later cosponsored [United Nations Security Council Resolution 2817](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/United_Nations_Security_Council_Resolution_2817), condemning Iran's retaliatory attacks on the Gulf states.

  [^cite_note-\:0-305]

#### Europe

-  [Albania](738.md): Prime Minister [Edi Rama](277805.md) expressed Albania's support for "the United States in militarily supporting Israel today under the leadership of President Donald Trump". He added that "we stand firm with Israel and with the peace-seeking brotherly Arab countries", and declared that Albania will designate the IRGC as a terrorist organization.

  [^cite_note-402]

  [^cite_note-westernbalkans-403]

  &#x20;While most European governments called for restraint following the escalation of violence in the Middle East, Albania emerged as one of the most outspoken supporters of the US-Israeli military action against Iran. This position reflects Albania's longstanding alignment with the United States, as well as its strained relations with Iran, which have been influenced by Albania's hosting of members of the exiled Iranian opposition group Mujahideen-e-Khalq (MEK) and Iran-sponsored cyberattacks against Albania.

  [^cite_note-404]

  &#x20;Albania later cosponsored [United Nations Security Council Resolution 2817](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/United_Nations_Security_Council_Resolution_2817), condemning Iran's retaliatory attacks on the Gulf states.

  [^cite_note-\:0-305]

-  [Andorra](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Andorra): The Minister of Foreign Affairs [Imma Tor Faus](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Imma_Tor_Faus) expressed solidarity with the victims of the war and reaffirmed Andorra's commitment with peace.

  [^cite_note-405]

  &#x20;The government also announced that it is following the situation of more than 30 Andorran citizens stranded in the UAE and Qatar.

  [^cite_note-406]

  &#x20;Andorra later cosponsored [United Nations Security Council Resolution 2817](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/United_Nations_Security_Council_Resolution_2817), condemning Iran's retaliatory attacks on the Gulf states.

  [^cite_note-\:0-305]

-  [Austria](26964606.md): Chancellor [Christian Stocker](78148335.md) condemned Iran's attacks on Persian Gulf nations, adding that the Iranian people deserve a "life in peace, security, and prosperity."

  [^cite_note-poleureactions-271]

  &#x20;Austria later cosponsored [United Nations Security Council Resolution 2817](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/United_Nations_Security_Council_Resolution_2817), condemning Iran's retaliatory attacks on the Gulf states.

  [^cite_note-\:0-305]

-  [Belarus](3457.md): President [Alexander Lukashenko](156589.md) called "Israel’s treacherous attack on Iran with U.S. support" "unacceptable" to Belarus.

  [^cite_note-407]

-  [Belgium](3343.md): Foreign Minister [Maxime Prévot](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Maxime_Pr%C3%A9vot) condemned the attacks by Iran on its neighbours in the "strongest possible terms", advising Belgian nationals in the regions to seek shelter.

  [^cite_note-poleureactions-271]

  &#x20;Belgium later cosponsored [United Nations Security Council Resolution 2817](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/United_Nations_Security_Council_Resolution_2817), condemning Iran's retaliatory attacks on the Gulf states.

  [^cite_note-\:0-305]

-  [Bosnia and Herzegovina](3463.md): Bosnia and Herzegovina cosponsored [United Nations Security Council Resolution 2817](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/United_Nations_Security_Council_Resolution_2817), condemning Iran's retaliatory attacks on the Gulf states.

  [^cite_note-\:0-305]

-  [Bulgaria](3415.md): The foreign ministry condemned the Iranian strikes on Gulf states, describing them as a 'dangerous military escalation' for which Iran bore responsibility. It also urged Iran to cease its military actions.

  [^cite_note-poleureactions-271]

  &#x20;Bulgaria later cosponsored [United Nations Security Council Resolution 2817](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/United_Nations_Security_Council_Resolution_2817), condemning Iran's retaliatory attacks on the Gulf states.

  [^cite_note-\:0-305]

-  [Croatia](5573.md): Foreign Minister [Gordan Grlić-Radman](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Gordan_Grli%C4%87-Radman) called for deescalation and condemned Iran's "intransigence and lack of credibility" which resulted in the attacks by the US and Israel.

  [^cite_note-poleureactions-271]

  &#x20;Croatia later cosponsored [United Nations Security Council Resolution 2817](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/United_Nations_Security_Council_Resolution_2817), condemning Iran's retaliatory attacks on the Gulf states.

  [^cite_note-\:0-305]

  Message (in Greek) from President of Cyprus, [Nikos Christodoulides](58652814.md), following the first Iranian strikes on Cyprus on 2 March 2026

-  [Cyprus](5593.md): President [Nikos Christodoulides](58652814.md) condemned Iranian drone and missile attacks against several Arab states, describing them as violations of international law. He emphasized Cyprus's commitment to regional stability and called for immediate de-escalation and a return to diplomatic channels.

  [^cite_note-poleureactions-271]

  &#x20;Also protests started in Cyprus against the existence of [British Cyprus bases](1025144.md), as the [Cyprus–United Kingdom relations](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Cyprus%E2%80%93United_Kingdom_relations) worsened, due to [Cypriots](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Cypriots) fearing that the country could be dragged into the Iranian conflict if the bases are used for pro-US/Israel offensive activities instead of their declared defensive purposes.

  [^cite_note-408]

  [^cite_note-409]

  [^cite_note-410]

  &#x20;Cyprus later cosponsored [United Nations Security Council Resolution 2817](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/United_Nations_Security_Council_Resolution_2817), condemning Iran's retaliatory attacks on the Gulf states.

  [^cite_note-\:0-305]

-  [Czech Republic](5321.md): Prime Minister [Andrej Babiš](40378134.md) declared the "uncontrollable [Iranian nuclear programme](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Iranian_nuclear_programme) and support for terrorism" a danger to the Czech Republic and to all of Europe, adding the Czech Republic stands by the US and Israel and expressed a belief that stability and peace will soon prevail in the region.

  [^cite_note-411]

  [^cite_note-412]

  &#x20;Further during 28 February 2026, Babiš had also labelled Iranian attacks on the various Arab states a breach of their sovereignty, given they did not participate in the initial strikes.

  [^cite_note-413]

  &#x20;Czech president [Petr Pavel](42272533.md) condemned [Iran's human rights violations](3156692.md), [support for terrorism](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Iran_and_state-sponsored_terrorism), and [backing of Russia's war in Ukraine](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Iran_and_the_Russo-Ukrainian_war_\(2022%E2%80%93present\)).

  [^cite_note-414]

  &#x20;The Czech Republic later cosponsored [United Nations Security Council Resolution 2817](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/United_Nations_Security_Council_Resolution_2817), condemning Iran's retaliatory attacks on the Gulf states.

  [^cite_note-\:0-305]

-  [Denmark](76972.md): On 28 February, Foreign Minister [Lars Løkke Rasmussen](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Lars_L%C3%B8kke_Rasmussen) stated, "Iran's past actions are completely unacceptable," and mentioned that Danish officials are "closely monitoring" developments in the Middle East.

  [^cite_note-poleureactions-271]

  &#x20;Denmark later cosponsored [United Nations Security Council Resolution 2817](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/United_Nations_Security_Council_Resolution_2817), condemning Iran's retaliatory attacks on the Gulf states.

  [^cite_note-\:0-305]

-  [Estonia](28222445.md): Foreign Minister [Margus Tsahkna](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Margus_Tsahkna) characterized the death of Ayatollah Khamenei as a strategic blow to the Russia–Iran alliance.

  [^cite_note-poleureactions-271]

  &#x20;Estonia later cosponsored [United Nations Security Council Resolution 2817](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/United_Nations_Security_Council_Resolution_2817), condemning Iran's retaliatory attacks on the Gulf states.

  &#x20;He said: A war with Iran could create a recession worse than Corona.

  [^cite_note-416]

  &#x20;Foreign Minister [Elina Valtonen](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Elina_Valtonen) posted on [X](9988187.md) that Khamenei's assassination opens a window for long-awaited change in the Middle East, adding that "Iran has provided [Russia](25391.md) with drones and missiles used in [its war of aggression against Ukraine](70149799.md). Its non-compliance with nuclear non-proliferation agreements has been a constant threat to world peace".

  [^cite_note-417]

  &#x20;Finland later cosponsored [United Nations Security Council Resolution 2817](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/United_Nations_Security_Council_Resolution_2817), condemning Iran's retaliatory attacks on the Gulf states.

-  [France](5843419.md): President [Emmanuel Macron](43671127.md) called for an emergency meeting of the [United Nations Security Council](31956.md).

  [^cite_note-418]

  &#x20;On 28 February 2026, Macron issued a joint statement with UK Prime Minister Keir Starmer and German Chancellor Friedrich Merz condemning Iranian attacks on regional countries.

  [^cite_note-419]

  &#x20;On 3 March, Macron said that France "cannot approve of" the strikes being conducted "outside of international law", and said that Iran bears primary responsibility, while also announcing movement of materiel into the Mediterranean.

  [^cite_note-420]

  &#x20;On 6 March, Macron condemned the Israeli attacks on Lebanon after an *"unacceptable attack"* on [UNIFIL](787766.md) peacekeepers during a missile attack against Iranian-backed Hezbollah.

  [^cite_note-421]

  &#x20;France later cosponsored [United Nations Security Council Resolution 2817](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/United_Nations_Security_Council_Resolution_2817), condemning Iran's retaliatory attacks on the Gulf states.

  [^cite_note-\:0-305]

-  [Germany](11867.md): Chancellor [Friedrich Merz](1967626.md) said that he was informed in advance and that deliberations were underway in a joint statement with France and the UK.

  [^cite_note-422]

  &#x20;Addressing concerns over the legality of military action against Iran, Merz acknowledged a "dilemma", noting that decades of reliance on international law and sanctions had proven "clearly ineffective" against the Iranian government.

  [^cite_note-423]

  &#x20;On 1 March, Merz labelled the Iranian government as a '[terrorist regime](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/State_terrorism)' responsible for decades of domestic oppression, stating that Germany shares the goal of the United States and Israel to end its 'terror'.

  [^cite_note-Psaropoulos-267]

  [^cite_note-424]

  &#x20;On 24 March, President [Frank-Walter Steinmeier](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Frank-Walter_Steinmeier) described the U.S.–Israeli military campaign against Iran as a "politically disastrous mistake" and a "violation of international law."

  [^cite_note-425]

  &#x20;On 27 April, Merz remarked that "A whole nation is being humiliated by the Iranian leadership" and criticized the U.S. for having "no truly convincing strategy,"

  [^cite_note-426]

  &#x20;prompting the U.S. to begin withdrawing its troops from Germany.

  [^cite_note-427]

  &#x20;Germany later cosponsored [United Nations Security Council Resolution 2817](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/United_Nations_Security_Council_Resolution_2817), condemning Iran's retaliatory attacks on the Gulf states.

  &#x20;Greece later cosponsored [United Nations Security Council Resolution 2817](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/United_Nations_Security_Council_Resolution_2817), condemning Iran's retaliatory attacks on the Gulf states.

  [^cite_note-poleureactions-271]

  &#x20;On 3 March 2026, the foreign ministers of Romania and Poland called on all parties involved in the escalating Middle East tensions to exercise maximum restraint, respect international law, and protect civilians.

  [^cite_note-Nicholls-2026-449]

  &#x20;accusing them of pushing the region into a "humanitarian, economic, and potentially even radiological disaster".

  [^cite_note-450]

  &#x20;In a 6 March statement, [Vladimir Putin](32817.md) expressed condolences over the death of Iran's Supreme Leader Ali Khamenei and declared the Russian support for Iran through the "need for an immediate cessation of hostilities, the abandonment of forceful solutions to problems around Iran and throughout the Middle East, and a swift return to the path of political and diplomatic resolution", as also reafirmed Russian neutrality by being in contact with leaders of the Gulf states to aid them.
