Prime Minister of Spain [Pedro Sánchez](43297512.md) (standing all the way to the left) condemned the actions of Israel and the US.

-  [Spain](26667.md): Prime Minister [Pedro Sánchez](43297512.md) stated his government's "rejection" of "the unilateral military action of the United States and Israel, which represents an escalation and contributes to a more uncertain and hostile international order" as well as of "the actions of the Iranian regime and the Revolutionary Guard", demanding "immediate de-escalation" and negotiations aimed at a "lasting political solution".

  [^cite_note-452]

  [^cite_note-González-453]

  &#x20;Such declarations provoked inner struggles with the pro-US/Israel far-right politicians from [PP](404727.md) and [Vox](41928488.md) (who accused him of subordinating [Spanish foreign policy](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Foreign_relations_of_Spain) to his own interests and of the [PSOE](272596.md) instead of the [free world](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Free_World) in a war against tyranny).

  [^cite_note-454]

  [^cite_note-455]

  &#x20;Similarly, the far-left criticised the hypocrisy of the "No War" [anti-imperialist](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Anti-imperialism) speeches when there's still [Spanish participation in the 2026 Iran war](82654191.md) through the [regional mobilizations](82611313.md) in [British Cyprus bases](1025144.md) and NATO missions in the Middle East that makes Spain "accomplice" of the [American imperialism](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/American_imperialism) and [Israeli irredentism](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Israeli_irredentism).

  [^cite_note-456]

  &#x20;The Spanish government denied the US the use of joint military bases at [Rota](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Naval_Station_Rota) and [Morón](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Mor%C3%B3n_Air_Base) to carry out attacks on Iran. Foreign Minister [José Manuel Albares](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Jos%C3%A9_Manuel_Albares) stated that "The bases are not being used – nor will they be used – for anything that is not in the agreement \[with the US], nor for anything that isn't covered by the UN charter."

  [^cite_note-Jones-457]

  [^cite_note-458]

  &#x20;Fifteen US aircraft were reportedly seen leaving the bases shortly afterward.

  [^cite_note-Jones-457]

  &#x20;In response, Trump threatened to halt all trade with Spain and even impose an embargo.

  [^cite_note-459]

  [^cite_note-460]

  &#x20;However Sánchez maintained its posture despite the risks of leaving Spain in a diplomatic isolation on Europe,

  [^cite_note-461]

  &#x20;declaring that the US-Israel war on Iran is a "big error" which only developed a conflict that is foreign to Europe and is dragging the [Eastern Mediterranean](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Eastern_Mediterranean) region to instability.

  [^cite_note-González-453]

  [^cite_note-462]

  [^cite_note-463]

  &#x20;On 7 March, Spain also condemned the Israeli invasion of Lebanon during the [2026 Lebanon war](82549840.md).

  [^cite_note-464]

  &#x20;On 11 March, Spain withdrew its ambassador to Israel permanently.

  [^cite_note-465]

  &#x20;Spain later cosponsored [United Nations Security Council Resolution 2817](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/United_Nations_Security_Council_Resolution_2817), condemning Iran's retaliatory attacks on the Gulf states.

  [^cite_note-\:0-305]

-  [Sweden](5058739.md): Prime Minister [Ulf Kristersson](3246360.md) denounced Iranian counterattacks and criticized the Iran's "oppression" of its own citizens. He further identified Iran's nuclear program and its support for militant groups as long-term "destabilizing factors" in the Middle East and Europe.

  [^cite_note-poleureactions-271]

  &#x20;Sweden later cosponsored [United Nations Security Council Resolution 2817](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/United_Nations_Security_Council_Resolution_2817), condemning Iran's retaliatory attacks on the Gulf states.

  [^cite_note-\:0-305]

-  [Ukraine](31750.md): President [Volodymyr Zelenskyy](59210523.md) supported the Israeli–US strikes, stating that "it is important that the United States is acting decisively. Whenever there is American resolve, global criminals weaken. This understanding must also come to the Russians", adding that "in the end, the Middle East region will become safer and more stable."

  [^cite_note-Nicholls-2026-449]

  &#x20;The foreign ministry said that "the cause of the current ⁠events is precisely ⁠the violence and impunity of the Iranian regime, ⁠in particular the killings and ⁠repression of ⁠peaceful protesters, which have become particularly widespread in recent ‌months."

  [^cite_note-Al_Jazeera_English-2026a-256]

  &#x20;Zelenskyy also alleges that Russia is supplying Iran with [Shahed drones](75153890.md) for use against the US and Israel.

  [^cite_note-466]

  &#x20;Ukraine later cosponsored [United Nations Security Council Resolution 2817](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/United_Nations_Security_Council_Resolution_2817), condemning Iran's retaliatory attacks on the Gulf states.

  [^cite_note-\:0-305]

-  [United Kingdom](31717.md): The Government urged de-escalation to avoid "wider regional conflict" and stated that it had bolstered "defensive capabilities" and that consular support was being offered to British citizens in the region.

  [^cite_note-Beale-467]

  &#x20;Prime Minister [Keir Starmer](18681491.md) confirmed that British planes are "in the sky" but "played no role" in the Israeli–US strikes, calling on Iran to refrain from further strikes and stop the "appalling violence and repression against the Iranian people".

  [^cite_note-468]

  &#x20;Starmer added that Iran had backed more than 20 "potentially lethal attacks" in the UK over the last year, and that "it is clear they must never be allowed to develop a nuclear weapon."

  [^cite_note-469]

  &#x20;The UK initially did not permit the US to use British military bases for the attack, including [RAF Fairford](475013.md) and the military bases on [Diego Garcia](8630.md).

  [^cite_note-470]

  &#x20;However, Starmer later agreed to let the US use British military bases for defensive purposes. He claimed this decision complies with international law.

  [^cite_note-471]

  &#x20;The government also stated that it had bolstered "defensive capabilities" in the region and that consular support was being offered to British citizens in the region.

  [^cite_note-Beale-467]

  &#x20;Foreign Secretary [Yvette Cooper](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Yvette_Cooper) accused Iran of trying to drag Lebanon into the war.

  [^cite_note-472]

  &#x20;Home Secretary [Shabana Mahmood](27266407.md) approved the request from the [Metropolitan Police](192450.md) to ban the annual Iran-linked [Quds Day](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Quds_Day) march from taking place in London.

  [^cite_note-473]

  &#x20;The United Kingdom later cosponsored [United Nations Security Council Resolution 2817](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/United_Nations_Security_Council_Resolution_2817), condemning Iran's retaliatory attacks on the Gulf states.

  [^cite_note-\:0-305]



[Pope Leo XIV](54520372.md), the [head](23056.md) of the [Catholic Church](606848.md) and [Vatican City](32408.md) warned: "I address to the parties involved a heartfelt appeal to assume the moral responsibility to stop the spiral of violence before it becomes an irreparable abyss!"

-  [Vatican City](32408.md): [Pope Leo XIV](54520372.md) called on the involved nations to stop what he called the "spiral of violence" and warned that the war could lead to a "tragedy of enormous proportions." He also warned: "I address to the parties involved a heartfelt appeal to assume the moral responsibility to stop the spiral of violence before it becomes an irreparable abyss!"

  [^cite_note-474]

  &#x20;[Blase Cupich](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Blase_Cupich), an American [Catholic Cardinal](6221.md) who is close with the [Pope](23056.md), referred to a video released by the White House of attacks on Iran mixed with Hollywood film clips as "horrifying" and "sickening".

  [^cite_note-475]

  &#x20;Pope Leo later rebuked those who wage war and have "hands full of blood," stating that God "does not listen to the prayers of those who wage war, but rejects them." This was an apparent rebuke of [Pete Hegseth](34076202.md), who prayed for violence against enemies who deserved "no mercy."

  [^cite_note-476]

  &#x20;Trump responded by calling on the pope to call out Iran for the [massacre](82036668.md) of "42,000 unarmed protesters."

  [^cite_note-477]

#### North America

-  [Antigua and Barbuda](951.md): Prime Minister [Gaston Browne](43045866.md) expressed concern at the escalation of the conflict and condemned Iran's strikes on its neighbours.

  [^cite_note-caribbeannations-478]

  &#x20;Antigua and Barbuda later cosponsored [United Nations Security Council Resolution 2817](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/United_Nations_Security_Council_Resolution_2817), condemning Iran's retaliatory attacks on the Gulf states.

  [^cite_note-\:0-305]

-  [Bahamas](3451.md): Prime Minister [Philip Davis](19387000.md) stated that the government is closely monitoring the situation, warning Bahamians that the war could harshly impact the [cost of living](522934.md) due to the country being dependent on international trade, now impacted by the conflict.

  [^cite_note-479]

  [^cite_note-480]

  &#x20;The Minister of Economic Affairs [Michael Halkitis](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Michael_Halkitis) said that the Bahamas hope for a return to normalcy, advising Bahamians in the region to seek shelter.

  [^cite_note-481]

  &#x20;The Bahamas later cosponsored [United Nations Security Council Resolution 2817](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/United_Nations_Security_Council_Resolution_2817), condemning Iran's retaliatory attacks on the Gulf states.

  [^cite_note-\:0-305]

-  [Barbados](3455.md): The government urged Iran to uphold respect for international law and halt attacks on its neighbours, while subscribing to UN chief Gutérres's demand on all parties to exercise restraint and avoid further escalation.

  [^cite_note-482]

  [^cite_note-483]

  &#x20;Barbados later cosponsored [United Nations Security Council Resolution 2817](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/United_Nations_Security_Council_Resolution_2817), condemning Iran's retaliatory attacks on the Gulf states.

  [^cite_note-\:0-305]

-  [Canada](5042916.md): Prime Minister [Mark Carney](13571049.md) expressed support for U.S. and Israeli air strikes on Iran, describing the country as "the principal source of instability and terror throughout the Middle East" and a threat to international security.

  [^cite_note-Politico-266]

  &#x20;Carney later said that he supported the strikes "with regret, because the current conflict is another example of the failure of the international order".

  [^cite_note-484]

  &#x20;Carney also said the US-Israeli attack on Iran appeared to be "inconsistent with international law."

  [^cite_note-485]

  &#x20;On 2 April, Canadian MP Costas Menegakis said that any deal that would leave the current government in Iran intact is extremely dangerous.

  [^cite_note-486]

  &#x20;On 16 April, Canada called for all sides to continue talks, while condemning Iran's repression of protesters and dissidents.

  [^cite_note-487]

  &#x20;Canada later cosponsored [United Nations Security Council Resolution 2817](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/United_Nations_Security_Council_Resolution_2817), condemning Iran's retaliatory attacks on the Gulf states.

  [^cite_note-\:0-305]

-  [Costa Rica](5551.md): Foreign Minister [Arnoldo André Tinoco](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Arnoldo_Andr%C3%A9_Tinoco) said that Costa Rica expects a "big conflict" following the American and Israeli attacks, adding that the war can disrupt global trade and travel.

  [^cite_note-crc-488]

  &#x20;André also expressed Costa Rica's "energetic condemnation" of the Iranian attacks against its neighbours.

  [^cite_note-crc-488]

  &#x20;Costa Rica later cosponsored [United Nations Security Council Resolution 2817](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/United_Nations_Security_Council_Resolution_2817), condemning Iran's retaliatory attacks on the Gulf states.

  [^cite_note-\:0-305]

-  [Cuba](5042481.md): President [Miguel Díaz-Canel](38630938.md) condemned the US and Israeli strikes.

  [^cite_note-BAEnews-489]

-  [Dominica](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Dominica): The government issued a statement expressing "deep concern" at the events in Iran, urging for restraint and the protection of civilians. The government also commended the governments of Saudi Arabia, the UAE, Bahrain, Qatar, and Kuwait, for demonstrating "leadership, restraint, and maturity."

  [^cite_note-490]

  &#x20;Dominica later cosponsored [United Nations Security Council Resolution 2817](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/United_Nations_Security_Council_Resolution_2817), condemning Iran's retaliatory attacks on the Gulf states.

  [^cite_note-\:0-305]

-  [Dominican Republic](8060.md): The government said it "strongly condemns" the Iranian strikes on its neighbours and called on all parties involved to show restraint and seek a peaceful solution.

  [^cite_note-491]

  &#x20;The Dominican Republic later cosponsored [United Nations Security Council Resolution 2817](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/United_Nations_Security_Council_Resolution_2817), condemning Iran's retaliatory attacks on the Gulf states.

  [^cite_note-\:0-305]

-  [Grenada](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Grenada): Grenada cosponsored [United Nations Security Council Resolution 2817](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/United_Nations_Security_Council_Resolution_2817), condemning Iran's retaliatory attacks on the Gulf states.

  [^cite_note-\:0-305]

-  [Guatemala](17238567.md): The government issued a statement reaffirming its commitment to the UN charter, highlighting international law, state sovereignty, territorial integrity, and peaceful solutions to conflicts.

  [^cite_note-492]

  &#x20;Guatemala later cosponsored [United Nations Security Council Resolution 2817](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/United_Nations_Security_Council_Resolution_2817), condemning Iran's retaliatory attacks on the Gulf states.

  [^cite_note-\:0-305]

-  [Haiti](13373.md): Haiti cosponsored [United Nations Security Council Resolution 2817](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/United_Nations_Security_Council_Resolution_2817), condemning Iran's retaliatory attacks on the Gulf states.

  [^cite_note-\:0-305]

-  [Honduras](13394.md): President [Nasry Asfura](39721295.md) avoided mentioning any country, but issued a statement on the conflict saying that Honduras supports "countries that defend their security", expressing solidarity with those who defend regional peace in the Middle East.

  [^cite_note-493]

  &#x20;Honduras later cosponsored [United Nations Security Council Resolution 2817](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/United_Nations_Security_Council_Resolution_2817), condemning Iran's retaliatory attacks on the Gulf states.

  [^cite_note-\:0-305]

-  [Jamaica](15660.md): Jamaica cosponsored [United Nations Security Council Resolution 2817](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/United_Nations_Security_Council_Resolution_2817), condemning Iran's retaliatory attacks on the Gulf states.

  [^cite_note-\:0-305]

-  [Mexico](3966054.md): President [Claudia Sheinbaum](57813311.md) condemned the use of military force to impose changes, further criticising the United Nations, which she said had "stopped doing its job."

  [^cite_note-494]

  &#x20;Sheinbaum also expressed support for Spanish Prime Minister Pedro Sánchez over Spain's rift with the US, saying that "\[Sánchez's] position to bet for peace is very respectable."

  [^cite_note-495]

-  [Nicaragua](21362.md): Co-president [Rosario Murillo](13577522.md) condemned the attacks on Iran and sent condolences over the "martyrdom of the Iranian people, of Ayatollah Khamenei, and his family."

  [^cite_note-496]

-  [Panama](22997.md): Panama cosponsored [United Nations Security Council Resolution 2817](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/United_Nations_Security_Council_Resolution_2817), condemning Iran's retaliatory attacks on the Gulf states.

  [^cite_note-\:0-305]

-  [Saint Kitts and Nevis](27198.md): Saint Kitts and Nevis cosponsored [United Nations Security Council Resolution 2817](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/United_Nations_Security_Council_Resolution_2817), condemning Iran's retaliatory attacks on the Gulf states.

  [^cite_note-\:0-305]

-  [Saint Lucia](27208.md): Saint Lucia cosponsored [United Nations Security Council Resolution 2817](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/United_Nations_Security_Council_Resolution_2817), condemning Iran's retaliatory attacks on the Gulf states.

  [^cite_note-\:0-305]

-  [Saint Vincent and the Grenadines](27228.md): Prime Minister [Godwin Friday](52936025.md) expressed concern at the impact of the war in the Caribbean region, and told citizens to "brace" for the economic consequences, saying that "the bombs may not fall here, but they have serious implications for us and for other countries around the region."

  [^cite_note-497]

  &#x20;Saint Vincent and the Grenadines later cosponsored [United Nations Security Council Resolution 2817](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/United_Nations_Security_Council_Resolution_2817), condemning Iran's retaliatory attacks on the Gulf states.

  [^cite_note-\:0-305]

-  [Trinidad and Tobago](3565457.md): Prime Minister [Kamla Persad-Bissessar](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Kamla_Persad-Bissessar) said that Trinidad and Tobago "supports the actions of the United States and Israel and its allies aimed at preventing oppressive regimes from acquiring nuclear weapons capabilities that would jeopardise international peace and security".

  [^cite_note-caribbeannations-478]

  &#x20;She called Iran's nuclear program a "global threat" and expressed solidarity with the countries attacked by Iran.

  [^cite_note-caribbeannations-478]

  &#x20;Trinidad and Tobago later cosponsored [United Nations Security Council Resolution 2817](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/United_Nations_Security_Council_Resolution_2817), condemning Iran's retaliatory attacks on the Gulf states.

  [^cite_note-\:0-305]

#### Oceania

-  [Australia](4689264.md): Prime Minister [Anthony Albanese](556745.md), in a joint statement with Foreign Minister [Penny Wong](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Penny_Wong) and Defence Minister [Richard Marles](4386805.md), expressed support for US and Israeli strikes, also stating Australia stood with "the brave people of Iran in their struggle against oppression".

  [^cite_note-498]

  &#x20;Australia [later granted](82634971.md) seven members of the [Iranian women's national football team](9896170.md), who had been competing in Australia, humanitarian visas to allow them to stay in the country following fears of their safety in returning to Iran after they refused to sing the Iranian national anthem before their first fixture.

  [^cite_note-499]

  &#x20;In the days that followed, five of them withdrew their asylum applications and indicated a wish to return to Iran; three of those who had initially chosen to remain returned on 14 March, and a fifth player withdrew her asylum request on 15 March. Australia later cosponsored [United Nations Security Council Resolution 2817](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/United_Nations_Security_Council_Resolution_2817), condemning Iran's retaliatory attacks on the Gulf states.

  [^cite_note-\:0-305]

-  [Cook Islands](7067.md): Prime Minister [Mark Brown](29659170.md) ordered senior officials to take measures to secure fuel supply and other essential goods and services for the islanders, while assuring that the country could be affected economically by the war due to being in the final link of the [global supply chain](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Global_supply_chain).

  [^cite_note-500]

-  [Federated States of Micronesia](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Federated_States_of_Micronesia): The Federated States of Micronesia cosponsored [United Nations Security Council Resolution 2817](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/United_Nations_Security_Council_Resolution_2817), condemning Iran's retaliatory attacks on the Gulf states.

  [^cite_note-\:0-305]

-  [Fiji](10707.md): Foreign Minister [Sakiasi Ditoka](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Sakiasi_Ditoka) called for restraint and urged for a peaceful solution in accordance with international law.

  [^cite_note-501]

  &#x20;The commander of the [Republic of Fiji Military Forces](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Republic_of_Fiji_Military_Forces) said that Fijian soldiers in the region were safe and told their families to remain calm, assuring that the military operations were far from the Fijian facilities.

  [^cite_note-502]

  &#x20;Fiji later cosponsored [United Nations Security Council Resolution 2817](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/United_Nations_Security_Council_Resolution_2817), condemning Iran's retaliatory attacks on the Gulf states.

  [^cite_note-\:0-305]

-  [Kiribati](16674.md): Kiribati cosponsored [United Nations Security Council Resolution 2817](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/United_Nations_Security_Council_Resolution_2817), condemning Iran's retaliatory attacks on the Gulf states.

  [^cite_note-\:0-305]

-  [Marshall Islands](19147.md): President [Hilda Heine](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Hilda_Heine) declared a 90-day state of emergency on 30 March in response to a fuel crisis exacerbated by the Iranian blockade of the Strait of Hormuz.

  [^cite_note-503]

  &#x20;The Marshall Islands later cosponsored [United Nations Security Council Resolution 2817](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/United_Nations_Security_Council_Resolution_2817), condemning Iran's retaliatory attacks on the Gulf states.

  [^cite_note-\:0-305]

-  [New Zealand](4913064.md): Prime Minister [Christopher Luxon](62303596.md) and Foreign Affairs Minister [Winston Peters](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Winston_Peters) in a joint statement said "New Zealand has consistently condemned Iran's Nuclear programme, its destablilising activities in the region and elsewhere, and its repression of its own people", acknowledging the US/Israeli strikes were "designed to prevent Iran from continuing to threaten international peace and security" and calling for a resumption of negotiations and adherence to international law.

  [^cite_note-504]

  &#x20;New Zealand later cosponsored [United Nations Security Council Resolution 2817](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/United_Nations_Security_Council_Resolution_2817), condemning Iran's retaliatory attacks on the Gulf states.

  [^cite_note-\:0-305]

-  [Palau](23372.md): President [Surangel Whipps Jr.](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Surangel_Whipps_Jr.) warned of serious economic consequences for the country as a result of the Iranian blockade of the Strait of Hormuz. Whipps added that fuel and diesel prices increased by 50% and also basic goods with a deep impact in daily lives of Palauans.

  [^cite_note-505]

  &#x20;Palau later cosponsored [United Nations Security Council Resolution 2817](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/United_Nations_Security_Council_Resolution_2817), condemning Iran's retaliatory attacks on the Gulf states.

  [^cite_note-\:0-305]

-  [Papua New Guinea](22943.md): Foreign Minister [Justin Tkatchenko](73758211.md) expressed support for the US and Israel, accusing Iran of harbouring terrorism. Prime Minister [James Marape](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/James_Marape) conveyed sympathies for Israel and the UAE, asking for a total restoration of peace in the Middle East and beyond.

  [^cite_note-506]

  &#x20;Following the open support by PNG to the US and Israel, [Jerry Singirok](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Jerry_Singirok), a former commander of the [Papua New Guinea Defence Force](23408.md), urged PM Marape to take a neutral stance due to risks that aligning with the US and Israel would mean for security of PNG, especially of service members at the recently upgraded [Lombrum Naval Base](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Lombrum_Naval_Base) on [Manus Island](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Manus_Island).

  [^cite_note-507]

  &#x20;Papua New Guinea later cosponsored [United Nations Security Council Resolution 2817](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/United_Nations_Security_Council_Resolution_2817), condemning Iran's retaliatory attacks on the Gulf states.

  [^cite_note-\:0-305]

-  [Tonga](30158.md): Tonga cosponsored [United Nations Security Council Resolution 2817](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/United_Nations_Security_Council_Resolution_2817), condemning Iran's retaliatory attacks on the Gulf states.

  [^cite_note-\:0-305]

-  [Tuvalu](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Tuvalu): Tuvalu cosponsored [United Nations Security Council Resolution 2817](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/United_Nations_Security_Council_Resolution_2817), condemning Iran's retaliatory attacks on the Gulf states.

  [^cite_note-\:0-305]

-  [Vanuatu](32443.md): A government spokesman said that Vanuatu maintains a non-aligned position about conflicts between "big countries", adding that Vanuatu supports peace, while warning citizens about the possible negative effects on local economy as a result of the war, including rising inflation.

  [^cite_note-508]

  &#x20;Vanuatu later cosponsored [United Nations Security Council Resolution 2817](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/United_Nations_Security_Council_Resolution_2817), condemning Iran's retaliatory attacks on the Gulf states.

  [^cite_note-\:0-305]

#### South America



[Pablo Quirno](81415098.md), the foreign minister of Argentina, stated his support for the US and Israel.

-  [Argentina](18951905.md): President [Javier Milei](56599840.md) said during a visit to New York City on 9 March that he is "proud to be the most Zionist president in the world", adding that because "Iran bombed us (Argentina) twice \[...] Iran is our enemy and we will win this war."

  [^cite_note-509]

  [^cite_note-510]

  &#x20;Foreign Minister [Pablo Quirno](81415098.md) expressed support for the US and Israel, saying that their military operations seek a "lasting stability in the world and security in the region".

  [^cite_note-511]

  [^cite_note-512]

  &#x20;On 2 April, Argentina expelled Iran's top diplomat, ordering him to leave their territory within 48 hours.

  [^cite_note-513]

  &#x20;Argentina later cosponsored [United Nations Security Council Resolution 2817](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/United_Nations_Security_Council_Resolution_2817), condemning Iran's retaliatory attacks on the Gulf states.

  [^cite_note-\:0-305]

  &#x20;See: *[Argentina in the 2026 Iran war](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Argentina_in_the_2026_Iran_war)*

-  [Bolivia](3462.md): The government issued a statement reaffirming Bolivia's commitment to peace and urging the international community to uphold the observance of human rights. The statement also advised Bolivians in the region to seek consular assistance.

  [^cite_note-514]

  &#x20;Bolivia later cosponsored [United Nations Security Council Resolution 2817](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/United_Nations_Security_Council_Resolution_2817), condemning Iran's retaliatory attacks on the Gulf states.

  [^cite_note-\:0-305]

-  [Brazil](3383.md): Brazil condemned the attacks on Iran.

  [^cite_note-Magalhaes-515]

  &#x20;The [Ministry of Foreign Affairs](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Ministry_of_Foreign_Affairs_\(Brazil\)) condemned and expressed "grave concern" for the attacks, urging all parties involved to "respect International Law and to exercise maximum restraint to prevent an escalation of hostilities and to ensure the protection of civilians and civilian infrastructure".

  [^cite_note-516]

  [^cite_note-Magalhaes-515]

-  [Chile](5489.md): The outgoing government, headed by [Gabriel Boric](47329497.md), condemned "the attacks against Iran perpetrated by the United States in conjunction with Israel, as well as the Iranian regime's response against Israel and [Gulf states](3088555.md)", expressed its concern about the military escalation in the region and called for an end to the violence, reaffirming its commitment to nuclear non-proliferation.

  [^cite_note-517]

  &#x20;Incoming president [José Antonio Kast](55052190.md) released a statement saying that Chile must align with democratic nations, expressing concern at civilian casualties in the strikes and the risks of the Iranian nuclear programme. Kast's office also condemned Iran's actions against its neighbours.

  [^cite_note-518]

  &#x20;Chile later cosponsored [United Nations Security Council Resolution 2817](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/United_Nations_Security_Council_Resolution_2817), condemning Iran's retaliatory attacks on the Gulf states.

  [^cite_note-\:0-305]

-  [Colombia](5222.md): President [Gustavo Petro](13391962.md) condemned the US and Israeli strikes on Iran and the assassination of Khamenei, accusing Prime Minister Netanyahu of "killing 108 schoolgirls" in one of the airstrikes.

  [^cite_note-519]

  &#x20;Petro later called for a "global front" against war and said that he will summon a peace conference to assure that the ongoing war will not hinder possibilities of a [Palestinian state](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Palestinian_state).

  [^cite_note-520]

-  [Ecuador](9334.md): The Foreign Ministry issued a statement condemning Iranian strikes on its neighbours.

  [^cite_note-521]

  &#x20;Ecuador later cosponsored [United Nations Security Council Resolution 2817](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/United_Nations_Security_Council_Resolution_2817), condemning Iran's retaliatory attacks on the Gulf states.

  [^cite_note-\:0-305]

-  [Guyana](17238691.md): The Foreign Ministry called for de-escalation and expressed solidarity with the Arab countries attacked by Iran, condemning Iranian strikes on them.

  [^cite_note-caribbeannations-478]

  &#x20;Guyana later cosponsored [United Nations Security Council Resolution 2817](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/United_Nations_Security_Council_Resolution_2817), condemning Iran's retaliatory attacks on the Gulf states.

  [^cite_note-\:0-305]

-  [Paraguay](23420.md): Foreign Minister [Rubén Ramírez Lezcano](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Rub%C3%A9n_Ram%C3%ADrez_Lezcano) expressed support for Israel during a phone call with Israeli FM [Gideon Sa'ar](485357.md), while President [Santiago Peña](58414156.md) condemned Iran's strikes on Arab neighbours.

  [^cite_note-522]

  &#x20;Peña later expressed support of the US-Israeli strikes against Iran and warned of the "failed world governance" imposed after World War II.

  [^cite_note-hORA-268]

  &#x20;Paraguay later cosponsored [United Nations Security Council Resolution 2817](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/United_Nations_Security_Council_Resolution_2817), condemning Iran's retaliatory attacks on the Gulf states.

  [^cite_note-\:0-305]

-  [Peru](170691.md): The government issued a statement condemning the Iranian strikes on its neighbours and called for peace in the region.

  [^cite_note-523]

  &#x20;Peru later cosponsored [United Nations Security Council Resolution 2817](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/United_Nations_Security_Council_Resolution_2817), condemning Iran's retaliatory attacks on the Gulf states.

  [^cite_note-\:0-305]

-  [Suriname](26828.md): Suriname cosponsored [United Nations Security Council Resolution 2817](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/United_Nations_Security_Council_Resolution_2817), condemning Iran's retaliatory attacks on the Gulf states.

  [^cite_note-\:0-305]

-  [Uruguay](31843.md): The [Ministry of Foreign Relations](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Ministry_of_Foreign_Relations_\(Uruguay\)) issued a statement expressing the Government's "grave concern" over the escalation of violence resulting from military operations conducted by the United States and Israel, as well as Iran's military response targeting neighbouring countries.

  [^cite_note-524]

  &#x20;The statement further noted that Uruguay, as a "country committed to the promotion of peace", urges the parties involved to resume diplomatic channels in order to address issues related to "the nuclear question".

  [^cite_note-525]

-  [Venezuela](32374.md): The [Foreign Ministry](68672022.md) condemned the strikes on Iran, calling them a "dangerous and unprecedented" escalation amid diplomatic talks to reach a nuclear deal. The message was later removed from social media after the confirmation of the death of Khamenei.

  [^cite_note-526]

  &#x20;Venezuela also condemned Iran's attacks on its neighbours as "undue and condemnable" and blamed a lack of dialogue in the region as a cause for the events.

  [^cite_note-BAEnews-489]

  [^cite_note-Al_Jazeera_English-2026a-256]

### Non-state actors

-  [Ahwaz Falcons](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Ahwaz_Falcons): The Ahwaz Falcons stated that "a new beginning for fieldwork in the occupied Arab land of Ahwaz", and threatened Iran, warning it "not to continue its crimes against our people and our occupied land of Ahwaz, and we also warn it not to continue its blatant interference in our Arab countries such as [Iraq](7515928.md), [Syria](7515849.md), [Yemen](350939.md), [Bahrain](18933277.md), and [Saudi Arabia](349303.md)".

  [^cite_note-527]

-  [Ahwaz Freedom Brigades](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Arab_separatism_in_Khuzestan#2026): The Ahwaz Freedom Brigades announced a rebellion and called on all of Ahwaz to join the rebellion.

  [^cite_note-528]

-  [Coordinating Council of Ahwazi Organizations](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Coordinating_Council_of_Ahwazi_Organizations): The Ahwaz Arab separatist organization accused Iran of forcibly annexing "non-Persian peoples" into the nation and voiced support for Ahwazi national unity and self-determination. They also announced potential plans for future attacks amid the war.

  [^cite_note-529]

- [Kurdistan Region](3949386.md) [Coalition of Political Forces of Iranian Kurdistan](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Coalition_of_Political_Forces_of_Iranian_Kurdistan): [Kurdistan Freedom Party](49010325.md) spokesman Hana Yazdanpana informed *[Middle East Eye](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Middle_East_Eye)* that the militant group supported the American and Israeli attacks on Iran.

  [^cite_note-530]

  &#x20;Representatives from the coalition stated that they were jointly coordinating political and military decisions and [preparing for a new phase](82575260.md), claiming that their forces were "deep inside Iran" and along the [Iran–Iraq border](10282111.md), ready to respond as the situation develops.

  [^cite_note-531]

-  Balochistan People's Party: The Iranian-Balochi federalist political party accused Iran of repression and adventurism, warning of greater oppression of ethnic minorities by the Iranian government if Israel and the United States both fail to topple it.

  [^cite_note-baloch-532]

-  [Cyber Jihad Movement](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Cyber_Jihad_Movement): Amid the 2026 Iran war, the [Al-Qaeda](1921.md)-affiliated hacking group released a statement in support of [pro-Iran hacking groups](82573356.md) against Israel and the United States.

  [^cite_note-Telegram2026-533]

  [^cite_note-DarkOwl2026-534]

  [^cite_note-GuruculIranCyberOps-535]

  [^cite_note-536]

-  [Free Balochistan Movement](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Free_Balochistan_Movement): The Baloch separatist group, which split from the militant organization [Baloch Liberation Army](6010135.md), welcomed the American-Israeli strikes on Iran and called for the safeguards of human rights and democracy.

  [^cite_note-537]

-  [Hamas](13913.md): Hamas in the [Gaza Strip](12047.md) condemned the "Zionist-American hostilities on the Islamic Republic" and affirmed its solidarity with Iran, further calling for [Muslim unity](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Pan-Islamism) in the face of US-Israeli attempts "to establish '[Greater Israel](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Greater_Israel)' at the expense of Arab and Muslim lands."

  [^cite_note-Lehmann-538]

  &#x20;Hamas later urged Iran to stop "avoid targeting neighboring countries" and expressed its "deep concern."

  [^cite_note-539]

-  [Harakat Hezbollah al-Nujaba](48027962.md): HNN Secretary-General [Akram al-Kaabi](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Akram_al-Kaabi) condemned the Israeli–American attacks on Iran and vowed to join the war, stating "our mujahideen and heroes are not those who speak in gatherings saying, 'if only we had been with you' and then they cower on the battlefield."

  [^cite_note-Toomey-540]

-  [Hezbollah](13919.md): Hezbollah in [Lebanon](17771.md) condemned the US-Israeli strikes, stating "we are confident that the American and Israeli enemy will receive a great slap and will reap nothing but failure from its tyrannical, criminal aggression."

  [^cite_note-El_Damanhoury-2026-541]

  &#x20;After the [assassination of Ali Khamenei](82537558.md) was confirmed, Hezbollah Secretary-General [Naim Qassem](28105310.md) vowed to retaliate and "undertake our duty of confronting the aggression", stating that Hezbollah would not leave "the field of honour and resistance". Hezbollah joined the war on 2 March [launching strikes on Israel](82549840.md).

  [^cite_note-542]

  &#x20;Israeli defense minister [Israel Katz](19041378.md) stated that Qassem is "marked for elimination".

  [^cite_note-543]

  &#x20;Hezbollah also condemned US, Israel attack on Iran as a “blatant violation of international law and the United Nations Charter”

  [^cite_note-Al_Jazeera_English-2026a-256]

-  [Houthi movement](17240332.md): The Houthi movement in [Yemen](350939.md) threatened to escalate their [conflict in the Red Sea](75207766.md).

  [^cite_note-544]

  &#x20;They decided to resume missile and drone attacks on US and Israeli-flagged ships in the [Red Sea](26191.md).

  [^cite_note-545]

  &#x20;Houthi leader [Abdul-Malik al-Houthi](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Abdul-Malik_al-Houthi) said that his forces are "in a state of high readiness for any necessary developments", adding that Iran's retaliatory strikes are a "legitimate right and is not a targeting of the countries in which the American bases are located".

  [^cite_note-El_Damanhoury-2026-541]

-  [Islamic State](9087364.md): In *[Al-Naba](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Al-Naba)*, the Islamic State framed the war against Iran as a “divinely sanctioned” conflict between two sides it regards as disbelievers.

  [^cite_note-546]

-  [Kata'ib Hezbollah](23626646.md): KH in [Iraq](7515928.md) threatened to "soon begin [attacking US bases](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Attacks_on_US_bases_during_the_Gaza_war) in response to their aggression" after a strike in [Jurf al-Nasr](16005462.md) area of Iraq's [Babylon Governorate](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Babylon_Governorate) killed two people and injured three others.

  [^cite_note-547]

  &#x20;KH also threatened a prolonged war with the United States, saying that "we must drag \[the United States] into a long war of attrition \[…] in which we leave no American presence in the region generally, especially in Iraq."

  [^cite_note-Toomey-540]

-  [Kata'ib Sayyid al-Shuhada](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Kata'ib_Sayyid_ul-Shuhada): KSS leader Abu Ala al-Walai justified the Iranian retaliatory strikes on neighboring Arab states, claiming that they only targeted American sites and personnel. He also claimed that U.S. personnel had "fled their bases scattered throughout the Gulf countries and sought refuge in hotels and civilian buildings" to prompt Iran to strike cities and civilian infrastructure in the Gulf.

  [^cite_note-Toomey-540]

-  [Kurdistan Region](3949386.md): [Nechirvan Barzani](1110441.md), President of the autonomous Kurdistan Region in [Iraq](7515928.md) expressed condolences for the martyrdom of the late Ayatollah Ali Khamenei, the Supreme Leader of the Islamic Republic of Iran, stating that "we sympathize and share in their grief, we pray to Almighty God to bless his soul with His mercy and kindness and to grant patience and comfort to everyone."

  [^cite_note-548]

  &#x20;Following the targeting of [Erbil International Airport](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Erbil_International_Airport) and the [Consulate General of the United States in Erbil](82535292.md) by Iranian missiles,

  [^cite_note-549]

  &#x20;a senior commander of the [Peshmerga](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Peshmerga) stated that his forces were on alert and "prepared for any eventuality".

  [^cite_note-550]

-  [Libyan National Army](35457665.md): [Khalifa Haftar](31227894.md), LNA Supreme Commander and leader of the [Government of National Stability](71621370.md), expressed his "strong condemnation of the blatant Iranian attacks targeting the UAE" stressing that "these attacks represent a serious violation and a direct threat to the stability of the region" and expressed full solidarity with the UAE.

  [^cite_note-551]

-  [National Guard](80827805.md): Sheikh [Hikmat al-Hijri](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Hikmat_al-Hijri) reaffirmed his support for Israel by calling the [Iran war](82533582.md) a "strategic direction of the allies", arguing that Iran has "destabilized the region" and harmed its own people.

  [^cite_note-552]

-  [Palestinian Islamic Jihad](13093253.md): PIJ condemned the strikes, stating that Iran has the right "to defend itself and deter opponents" and that "these hostilities target not only Iran, but all countries and people of our nation, and seek to impose domination by war criminals."

  [^cite_note-Lehmann-538]

-  [People's Fighters Front](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/People's_Fighters_Front): PFF released a statement calling the Iranian government "oppressive" and "incompetent", holding it responsible for the "devastating war". The Council of Scholars said that the Iranian people played no part in bringing war from Israel or the US and vowed further conflicts against the Iranian government.

  [^cite_note-baloch-532]

- [Sahrawi Arab Democratic Republic](38762388.md) [Polisario Front](201574.md): Polisario Front representative to Spain Abdulah Arabi condemned the Spanish government's lack of "coherence" by condemning the war on Iran but while not supporting [Sahrawi independence](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Sahrawi_nationalism), stating that while countries must uphold international law, "they must also be consistent in their defense of international law with regard to [Western Sahara](33220.md)."

  [^cite_note-553]

-  [Popular Mobilization Forces](45671374.md): The PMF condemned U.S. airstrikes targeting its several headquarters and bases across Iraq, condemning them as "sinful air attacks" and a "blatant transgression" and a serious violation of Iraq's sovereignty.

  [^cite_note-554]

### Intergovernmental organizations

See also: [United Nations Security Council Resolution 2817](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/United_Nations_Security_Council_Resolution_2817)

-  [Arab League](52625.md): Arab League Secretary-General [Ahmed Aboul Gheit](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Ahmed_Aboul_Gheit) condemned the Iranian attacks on several Arab states, calling them "fully reprehensible" and a "grave Iranian strategic mistake," constituting "not only a blatant violation of international law and the UN charter, but also an assault on the principles of good neighborliness." An Arab League emergency meeting has been scheduled for 8 March 2026.

  [^cite_note-555]

-  [European Union](9317.md): European Commission President ⁠[Ursula von der Leyen](2991932.md) and Council ⁠President [António Costa](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Ant%C3%B3nio_Costa) called the conflict "greatly concerning" and urged restraint.

  [^cite_note-Al_Jazeera_English-2026a-256]

  &#x20;On 1 March 2026, von der Leyen stated that she supported a "credible transition" of power in Iran.

  [^cite_note-Europa-257]

  [^cite_note-Zsiros-258]

  &#x20;The EU officially called for de-escalation and diplomatic initiatives with the help of the Gulf countries, adjacent [regions in Africa](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Northeast_Africa) and the [Indo-Pacific](330456.md) that are also affected by the War.

  [^cite_note-556]

-  [Gulf Cooperation Council](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Cooperation_Council_for_the_Arab_States_of_the_Gulf): The GCC convened an emergency council meeting and ordered Iran to cease attacks on Arab countries immediately.

  [^cite_note-557]

- [United Nations](31769.md) [International Atomic Energy Agency](14984.md): The IAEA convened an emergency meeting requested by the [Russian Federation](25391.md) on 2 March.

  [^cite_note-558]

  &#x20;In March 2026, IAEA Director General [Rafael Grossi](62195943.md) reported that while Iran maintained a significant stockpile of near-weapons-grade uranium, there was no evidence of a structured program to build a nuclear bomb.

  [^cite_note-559]

  &#x20;The IAEA confirmed on 17 March 2026 that a projectile hit the premises of the [Bushehr Nuclear Power Plant](18522794.md) in southwestern Iran, stating that no damage to the facility or injuries to staff were reported.

  [^cite_note-560]

-  [NATO](21133.md): NATO is a defense organization of member states that agree to mutual defence in response to an attack on a member state by an outside party, for example, their responses to the 9-11 attack upon the United States, not an agreement to join an aggression conducted by a member state.

  [^cite_note-561]

  &#x20;A NATO spokesperson stated they were closely watching the situation while a senior official told [*Stars and Stripes*](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Stars_and_Stripes_\(newspaper\)) that its missile defence vigilance was heightened in order to protect its member states in the event that Iranian attacks expanded.

  [^cite_note-Korte-261]

  [^cite_note-Bayer-262]

  &#x20;[Mark Rutte](4321056.md), NATO secretary general, said in an interview with BBC that Europe is "supportive" of the United States attacks on Iran stating that Iran is a "threat".

  [^cite_note-BBC-263]

  &#x20;Trump thanked NATO Secretary General Mark Rutte for his support.

  [^cite_note-562]

- The [Shanghai Cooperation Organization](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Shanghai_Cooperation_Organization) expressed serious concern over the conflict. The statement also stated that member states of the SCO consider the use of force as unacceptable.

  [^cite_note-563]

- [UNHRC](635790.md): The UNHRC issued a strong condemnation regarding Iran's attack on regional countries, calling it to immediately compensate them for the damages it caused.

  [^cite_note-564]

- [Red cross](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Red_cross): Mirjana Spoljaric, president of the International Committee of the Red Cross, called on countries to respect the rules of war and urged them to find the political will to prevent “further death and destruction”. She warned that “a dangerous chain reaction” of military escalation was under way across the Middle East, “with potentially devastating consequences for civilians”.

  [^cite_note-Al_Jazeera_English-2026a-256]

## Media coverage

### Iran media coverage

Before hostilities erupted, media attention on US-Iran tensions increased in early 2026, following failed nuclear talks and [domestic unrest in Iran](81976180.md). Iranian state media, such as the [IRNA](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/IRNA), portrayed these developments as provocations.[^cite_note-Brookings_Institute-03032026-565]

[Lego](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Lego)-style [propaganda](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Propaganda) animation produced by Iranian media company [Explosive Media](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Explosive_Media) using [text-to-video modeling](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Text-to-video_model).

Iranian state-controlled outlets have been described as employing propaganda techniques, including the use of [AI-generated videos](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/AI-generated_video) (such as those produced by [Explosive Media](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Explosive_Media)) and inflated casualty figures.[^cite_note-566] Trump has accused Iran of using artificial intelligence as a "disinformation weapon."[^cite_note-567] Iranian media broadcast manipulated images of alleged victories, prompting fact-checking from organizations like [NewsGuard](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/NewsGuard).[^cite_note-CNN-05032026-568] The Iranian government responded to the [Trump administration](78298108.md)'s use of video game clips, cartoon characters, and blockbuster movie scenes for Iran messaging with an AI-generated [propaganda video](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Propaganda_video) mimicking [Lego animation](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Brickfilm), produced by the Revayat-e-Fath Institute and redistributed on various government-run outlets, including redistribution by the [Iranian Ministry of Foreign Affairs](1166971.md). The video declares itself in remembrance of the [Minab school attack](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Minab_school_attack) and showcases Iran attacking various of its neighbours and American-Israeli outposts.[^cite_note-569][^cite_note-570]

### Media coverage in Israel

Since the signing of the Islamabad Memorandum, the Israeli reactions to Trump and the declared end of hostilities towards Iran had been largely negative, with many expressing feelings of outrage and betrayal. For instance, *Israel Hayom* wrote in its headline "You could have been the greatest president of all, but you failed" and accused Trump of signing a surrender agreement and having humiliated his own country.[^cite_note-571][^cite_note-572][^cite_note-573]

### US media coverage

Before hostilities erupted, media attention on US-Iran tensions increased in early 2026, following failed nuclear talks and reports of Iranian domestic unrest. Outlets such as [CNN](62028.md) and *[The New York Times](30680.md)* detailed US military buildups in the region, speculating about possible attacks.[^cite_note-574] As the airstrikes began, initial reporting relied heavily on satellite imagery and official briefings. The Pentagon's announcements of the destruction of Iranian assets were widely echoed, but independent verification proved difficult due to Tehran's communications restrictions.[^cite_note-575] *[Slate](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Slate_\(magazine\))* criticized American news coverage of the war, arguing that headlines too often treat statements from the Trump administration as objective and fail to also cover points of contradiction by Iran.[^cite_note-576]

#### Video game clips



Map posted by Trump on [Truth Social](69077351.md) of the US flag covering Iran, hinting annexation

The White House faced backlash over a video that mixed real war footage with video game clips.[^cite_note-CNN-05032026-568] *[Mondoweiss](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Mondoweiss)* said such efforts distorted public understanding.[^cite_note-Mondoweiss-04032026-577] [PBS](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/PBS) argued [The Trump administration's use](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Use_of_copyrighted_works_by_the_second_Trump_administration) of video game footage, cartoon and blockbuster movie scenes for Iran messaging "risks reducing a real war to spectacle".[^cite_note-578] Trump has repeatedly shared provocative images regarding the Iran war such as a map of the US flag over Iran with words reading "United States of the Middle East?" and an AI-generated image of Iranian ships being bombed.[^cite_note-579][^cite_note-580]

The White House circulates videos fusing real airstrike footages with visuals from video games and action movies, "transforming acts of destruction into a spectacle of national triumph", keeping the audience detached from the human suffering it produces.[^cite_note-581] Artists and entertainment companies often object to their works use in such clips. [Kesha](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Kesha)'s song "[Blow](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Blow_\(Kesha_song\))" was used alongside footage of an airstrike and the caption "Lethality", which prompted the singer to post on X, "Stop using my music, perverts @WhiteHouse".[^cite_note-582] [Blase Cupich](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Blase_Cupich), an American [Catholic Cardinal](6221.md) who is close with [Pope Leo XIV](54520372.md), referred to a video released by the White House of attacks on Iran mixed with Hollywood film clips as "horrifying" and "sickening".[^cite_note-583]

#### US government reactions

On 14 March 2026, [Federal Communications Commission](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Federal_Communications_Commission) chairman [Brendan Carr](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Brendan_Carr), a Trump appointee, warned broadcasters that those who "perpetrate fake news and distort the news" must "take the right course" or risk losing their licenses at renewal.[^cite_note-584][^cite_note-585] Carr said broadcasters "must act in the public interest, and if they don't, they will lose their licenses," citing the public's low trust in legacy media and past election coverage problems. Trump expressed that he was "enthusiastic" about Carr's stance.[^cite_note-586] In response to opposition of the war from Pope Leo XIV, Trump falsely claimed that the pope supported Iran having nuclear weapons and shared provocative memes on social media.[^cite_note-587]

Defense Secretary [Pete Hegseth](34076202.md) also criticized the coverage during a news conference, taking issue with CNN's reporting and suggesting emphasizing Iranian frustration rather than adding to the headlines.[^cite_note-588] [Democratic](5043544.md) lawmakers condemned the FCC's remarks as unconstitutional censorship, with many calling it "oppressive" and a violation of the First Amendment.[^cite_note-589]

On 3 June 2026, the [US House](19468510.md) passed an act with a vote of 215-208 requiring the United States' war efforts to cease or gain approval from [Congress](31756.md) to continue fighting.[^cite_note-590]

On 23 June 2026, the [US Senate](24909346.md) enacted an act by a vote of 50-48 mandating the United States to either end its war operations or seek congressional consent to continue fighting.[^cite_note-591]

Azerbaijani president [Ilham Aliyev](22765442.md) visiting the Iranian Embassy in Baku to offer condolences for the death of Ayatollah Ali Khamenei, 4 March 2026

### International media coverage

News media and traders have repeatedly applied the pejorative abbreviation "[Trump Always Chickens Out](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Trump_Always_Chickens_Out)" (TACO), previously used first for the [Liberation Day tariffs](79617151.md), for Trump's repeated refusals to enforce his "red line" threats against Iran. Another pejorative abbreviation called "[Not A Chance Hormuz Opens](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Not_A_Chance_Hormuz_Opens)" (NACHO) was devised in reference to Trump's inability to quickly reopen the strait.[^cite_note-592][^cite_note-593] *[CNN](62028.md)* observed that over the course of the war, Trump's repeated talking points, such as his claim that Iran's military had been destroyed and another that Iranian leadership supposedly really wants to make a deal with Trump regarding the war, had been stated baselessly for months.[^cite_note-594] *[The Atlantic](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/The_Atlantic)* argued that it was Trump who was visibly desperate for a deal and that Iran is able to exploit his needs.[^cite_note-595] *[Al-Monitor](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Al-Monitor)* reported that Netanyahu and his inner political circle view Trump's insistence on a deal as disastrous and blame him for failing to achieve regime change and victory against Iran.[^cite_note-596]

New reports have determined suspicious [insider trading](15368.md) patterns within the stock and oil markets in correlation with "positive" news of the war from Trump over several months.[^cite_note-597][^cite_note-598][^cite_note-599] [Wall Street](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Wall_Street) analysts and Iranian state media had accused journalist [Barak Ravid](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Barak_Ravid) of *[Axios](52436164.md)* of repeating false information from anonymous sources regarding ongoing negotiations to allow for [market manipulation](12130673.md) of oil prices.[^cite_note-600][^cite_note-601] *[The Wall Street Journal](173070.md)* argued that Trump directly intervened in the oil market by "[jawboning](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Jawboning)" it, posting "positive" news of supposed negotiations to kill momentum of oil price increases by traders in quick responses to "negative" news.[^cite_note-602] *CNN* observed that Trump incorrectly claimed that the US was close to an agreement with Iran to end the war more than 35 times from late March to early June, many of which involved Trump portraying Iran as supposedly "desperate".[^cite_note-false-603] Because of the volatility of the oil market as a result of Trump's frequent social media posts affecting them, investors had reported fatigue and became increasingly driven away. These trends caused a near 17% decline in brent crude oil [open interest](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Open_interest) by June, the fastest since 2009.[^cite_note-604]

Analysts accused Western media of failing to challenge the official narrative, with [Mondoweiss](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Mondoweiss) highlighting the flattering language that downplayed civilian casualties.[^cite_note-Mondoweiss-04032026-577] [Al Jazeera English](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Al_Jazeera_English) noted the overrepresentation of voices from the pro-war [Iranian diaspora](9573713.md).[^cite_note-Al_Jazeera_English-07032026-605] Pre-war coverage was also marred by inadequate public warnings.[^cite_note-606]
